Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any autoimmunity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 183. The projective resolution has length at most 12 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 183 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the autoimmunity structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 12. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
